{"forms":[{"form":"the Liouville-Arnold theorem","tags":["canonical"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["In dynamical systems theory, a theorem stating that if, in a Hamiltonian dynamical system with n degrees of freedom, there are also known n first integrals of motion that are independent and in involution, then there exists a canonical transformation to action-angle coordinates in which the transformed Hamiltonian is dependent only upon the action coordinates and the angle coordinates evolve linearly in time.
